<div className="type-signature"><span className="modifiers">public class </span><span className="element-name type-name-label">GPUdb</span> <span className="extends-implements">extends <a href="/content/api/java/com/gpudb/GPUdbBase" title="class in com.gpudb">GPUdbBase</a></span></div>
